## ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
## 2b. BEHAVIOUR: builds a Roblox GUI (Frame/TextLabel/TextButton/ScreenGui,
##     UIPadding/UIScale), Path2D curve visuals (SetControlPoints,
##     GetPositionOnCurve/GetTangentOnCurve/GetLength), Random/Shuffle,
##     getfenv/debug.getinfo/identifyexecutor. Network-capable names present
##     (HttpService/GetAsync/PostAsync/RequestAsync) but NO hardcoded endpoint.
##     Not observed: writefile of secrets, remote-loadstring, HWID exfil, webhook.

## ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
## 2c. REGISTER-LEVEL LIFTED LUA (devirt/lift.py; 76 protos, 8,224 instrs,
##     98% opcodes; 920 runtime + 170 decoded constants inlined; VALIDATED:
##     parses as Lua 5.4). e[]=registers, K(i)=constant in cold/unexecuted
##     code. Faithful transliteration — NOT clean source, NOT runnable as-is.
##     Runnable payload = unpacked_runnable.lua.
## ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
